# Commission Implementing Decision (EU) 2026/1453 of 2 July 2026 correcting Commission Implementing Decision (EU) 2024/411 on the list of shipping companies specifying the administering authority in respect of a shipping company in accordance with Directive 2003/87/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council

**COMMISSION IMPLEMENTING DECISION (EU) 2026/1453**

**of 2 July 2026**

**correcting Commission Implementing Decision (EU) 2024/411 on the list of shipping companies specifying the administering authority in respect of a shipping company in accordance with Directive 2003/87/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council**

**(Text with EEA relevance)**

THE EUROPEAN COMMISSION,

Having regard to the Treaty on the Functioning of the European Union,

Having regard to Directive 2003/87/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council of 13 October 2003 establishing a system for greenhouse gas emission allowance trading within the Union and amending Council Directive 96/61/EC (1), and in particular Article 3gf(2), point (b), thereof,

Whereas:

(1) The Annex to Commission Implementing Decision (EU) 2024/411 (2) does not accurately reflect attributions of four shipping companies nor changes to the IMO numbers of 21 shipping companies recorded in Thetis-MRV before 11 August 2025. To ensure consistency in the format of the data, entries recorded in a manner deviating from the IMO identification scheme should be left blank.

(2) Implementing Decision (EU) 2024/411 should, therefore, be corrected to reflect those reattributions and the corresponding updates to the IMO numbers of the shipping companies,

HAS ADOPTED THIS DECISION:

## Article 1

The Annex to Implementing Decision (EU) 2024/411 is replaced by the text set out in the Annex to this Decision.

## Article 2

This Regulation shall enter into force on the twentieth day following that of its publication in the Official Journal of the European Union.

Done at Brussels, 2 July 2026.

For the Commission

The President

Ursula VON DER LEYEN

(1) OJ L 275, 25.10.2003, p. 32, ELI: http://data.europa.eu/eli/dir/2003/87/oj.

(2) Commission Implementing Decision (EU) 2024/411 of 30 January 2024 on the list of shipping companies specifying the administering authority in respect of a shipping company in accordance with Directive 2003/87/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council (OJ L, 2024/411, 31.1.2024, ELI: http://data.europa.eu/eli/dec_impl/2024/411/oj).
